In this report we describe a three-stage fine-tuning curriculum applied to Qwen3-27B that is designed to progressively specialize the model for the C-to-Rust (C2Rust) translation task: (1) continued pretraining on Rust-centric corpora to strengthen the model's prior over idiomatic Rust syntax and standard-library usage; (2) supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on the microsoft/Verus_Training_Data dataset to instill debugging and self-repair behavior over Rust code; and (3) task-specific SFT on paired C/Rust solutions derived from LeetCode problems to teach direct semantic translation. We evaluate the resulting model using the agentic, static-analysis-guided verification framework of SACTOR, which performs structure-aware, two-phase (unidiomatic to idiomatic) translation with foreign-function-interface (FFI)-based end-to-end (E2E) testing. We report success rate, idiomaticity (Clippy lint counts, unsafe-code fraction), and failure-mode analyses, and compare our fine-tuned model against baseline Qwen3-27B and other LLMs evaluated under the same framework.

</details>


### [192] [Building AI-Intensive Software with AI: Early Results and a Cautionary Tale on Measuring Development Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13730)
*Victor Barros de Miranda Neves,Kiev Santos da Gama,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项关于AI密集型软件开发生态成本的案例研究：一个六人学生团队用一个学期构建了完整的对话式入职助手。最初报告的成本比率为19.4倍，但后续审查发现两个独立错误（在包月订阅下错误推断按token计费、使用错误的区域劳动力价格估算反事实），修正后约为9.9倍。作者将这种修正本身作为早期可推广的发现：两种错误都容易犯、不会在最终数字中显现、在类似报告中可能普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于AI密集型软件开发的真实成本的实证报告稀缺，而现有的少数报告容易被算错，且这些错误不会在最终数字中显现。作者希望通过案例研究提供更准确的成本数据，并揭示成本核算中常见的隐蔽错误。

Method: 采用正在进行中的案例研究方法：六人学生团队在一个学术学期内使用广泛的AI辅助构建了完整对话式入职助手（包括RAG代码聊天、导览、依赖图、技术债务分析）。开发过程用三层成本模型计量：真实AI支出、自我报告的人力投入、人类反事实成本。随后对初始报告进行审查修正。

Result: 初始报告的19.4倍成本比率在后续审查中因两个独立错误被修正为约9.9倍（错误将比率高估了约2倍）。两个错误分别是：在包月订阅场景下错误地推断按token计费的成本，以及使用错误的区域劳动力价格来计算反事实成本。

Conclusion: 成本修正本身是一个早期、可推广的发现：这两种计数错误容易犯、在最终数字中不可见，且很可能在类似的AI开发成本报告中普遍存在。作者提出下一步将建立更稳健、可复制的成本计量方法论。

Abstract: Empirical reports on the true cost of AI-intensive software development remain scarce, and the few that exist are easy to get wrong in ways that never surface in the final number. We report early results from an ongoing case study: a six-person student team built a full conversational onboarding assistant -- RAG-based code chat, guided tours, dependency graphs, technical-debt analysis -- over one academic term using pervasive AI assistance. We instrumented development with a three-layer cost model (real AI spend, self-reported human effort, human counterfactual) and initially reported a 19.4x cost ratio. A follow-up pass revealed two independent errors -- inferring per-token cost under a flat-rate subscription, and pricing the counterfactual with the wrong regional labor rates -- that together had inflated the ratio by roughly 2x; the corrected figure is ~9.9x. We present this correction as an early, generalizable finding in its own right: both errors are easy to make, invisible in the final number, and plausibly common in similar reports. We outline next steps toward a more robust, replicable costing methodology for AI-intensive development.

</details>


### [193] [Does ISO-Grounded NFR Specification Improve LLM Code Generation? A Comparison of Rich and Structured Interventions against a Natural-Language Baseline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13742)
*Joào Pedro Monteiro Pereira,Vinicius Cardoso Garcia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在LLM代码生成中，将非功能需求（NFR）规范基于ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行丰富化（无论是自然语言散文NL-rich还是结构化JSON格式），相比简单的单行基线（NL-simple）是否能提升代码生成质量。主要发现是ISO语义丰富化能改善静态质量代理指标并降低对提示词措辞的敏感性，但并不能可靠地提升功能正确性；当ISO内容恒定时，NL-rich和Structured在正确性上几乎无差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM代码生成中，NFR通常以简略的单行短语形式指定，作者想探究将这些规范基于ISO/IEC 25010质量模型进行语义丰富化是否能改善生成的代码质量，以帮助实践者决定如何在提示词中表达NFR。

Method: 在HumanEval/HumanEval-ET基准上评估四种NFR（性能、错误处理、代码异味、可读性），每种条件下使用十个提示变体，固定模型快照并采用配对非参数统计分析，比较三种条件：NL-simple（单行基线）、NL-rich（丰富自然语言散文）、Structured（结构化JSON）。

Result: ISO语义丰富化改善了静态质量代理指标（例如性能NFR的不可读性密度从0.88降至0.69），并降低了提示词措辞的敏感性，但未能可靠提升功能正确性；错误处理NFR的扩展测试通过率反而下降。当ISO内容恒定时，NL-rich和Structured在正确性方面的差异可忽略（|delta| <= 0.023）。

Conclusion: 实践者应投资于基于标准（ISO/IEC 25010）的NFR语义内容，而非关注序列化形式（JSON vs 散文）。要注意ISO丰富化在改善静态质量的同时可能与精确输出基准存在张力，尤其对防御性编码模式（如错误处理）而言。论文提供了完全可追踪的复现包。

Abstract: In LLM-based code generation, Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) are often specified as terse one-line phrases. We ask whether grounding those specifications in ISO/IEC 25010 Quality Model, either as rich natural-language prose (NL-rich) or as structured JSON (Structured), improves code generated on HumanEval/HumanEval-ET compared to a RobuNFR-style one-line baseline (NL-simple). We evaluate four NFRs (performance, error handling, code smell, readability) with ten prompt variations per condition under a fixed model snapshot and paired non-parametric analysis. Primary finding: ISO-grounded enrichment improves static quality proxies (unreadability density falls across all four NFRs (e.g., Performance 0.88 -> 0.69 for NL-rich)) and reduces sensitivity to prompt wording, but does not reliably improve functional correctness; for error handling, extended-test pass rate decreases, suggesting tension between defensive coding patterns and exact-output benchmarks. Secondary finding: when ISO content is held constant, NL-rich and Structured differ negligibly in correctness (|delta| <= 0.023), indicating that semantic content matters more than JSON-vs-prose format. Practitioners should invest in standard-grounded NFR content rather than serialization form. A fully traceable replication package is provided.

</details>


### [194] [Engineering Reliable Coding Agents: Evaluating and Operating the System Around the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13867)
*Stephanie Jarmak*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文档是一份系统级综述，探讨AI编码智能体的可靠性问题，指出其不仅取决于模型能力，还取决于外围系统组件（如执行环境、检索、状态管理等）。通过综合164篇学术文献、100条实践记录、29条基准记录和17个作者系统案例，提出了一套评估与运营编码智能体的框架，并贡献了206条可靠性记录、证据账本及可运行的评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体通常以模型形式被评估，但实际以系统形式部署。其可靠性不仅依赖模型能力，还依赖执行环境、检索、内存、权限、审查接口和资源分配等系统层面。许多表面上的模型失败实际上源于系统其他部分，而单层改进往往无法传导至端到端结果，因此需要系统级视角来区分模型能力与基础设施效应。

Method: 采用结构化多声部综述（multivocal review）方法，综合164篇学术文献、100条实践记录、29条基准记录和17个作者系统案例；辅以定向更新审计、软件工程覆盖分析、分布式系统证据综合；将评估与运营视为依赖链，构建版本化可靠性记录目录（206条），包括193条门控实践和13条研究线索。

Result: 产出包括：版本化的206条可靠性记录目录（含56条深度开发的实践）；证据账本；跨智能体生命周期的依赖与修复不对称框架；运营智能体系统的测量与失败案例；可运行的评估与可靠性协议；以及五个带证据映射的可复用智能体技能。

Conclusion: 本文档提供了一套系统级方法论，用于区分模型能力与基础设施效应、设计有说服力的评估、以及在组件故障时构建可安全恢复的系统。同时明确指出该综述是结构化而非穷尽式的，证据强度因主题而异，结果取决于工作负载与配置，并记录了搜索通道的执行状态与局限性。

Abstract: AI coding agents are commonly evaluated as models but deployed as systems. Their reliability depends not only on model capability, but on the harness, execution state, retrieval, memory and state management, permissions, review interfaces, and resource allocation. This monograph examines those boundaries and develops a framework for evaluating and operating coding agents reliably. It synthesizes 164 scholarly works, 100 practitioner records, 29 benchmark records, and 17 author-system case records through a structured multivocal review, targeted update audits, software-engineering coverage analysis, and distributed-systems evidence synthesis. Across this evidence, many apparent model failures originate elsewhere in the system, while improvements at one layer often fail to propagate to end-to-end outcomes. Evaluation and operation are treated as a dependency chain in which weaknesses in task construction, execution environments, retrieval, state management, verification, or observability can invalidate downstream conclusions. The monograph contributes a versioned catalog of 206 reliability records: 193 gated practices, including 56 developed in depth, plus 13 research leads; an evidence ledger; a framework for dependency and repair asymmetry across the agent lifecycle; measurements and failure cases from operated agent systems; runnable evaluation and reliability protocols; and five reusable agent skills with evidence maps. Together, these provide a system-level methodology for distinguishing model capability from infrastructure effects, designing defensible evaluations, and building systems that recover safely when components fail. The review is structured rather than exhaustive, evidence strength varies by topic, and results depend on workload and configuration. The methods record which search lanes were executed, which remain unexecuted, and limits on evidence-grading claims.

</details>


### [195] [Engineering Signals of Human-AI Collaboration in the Agentic Coding Era: A Longitudinal Analysis of 33,228 Pull Requests from vLLM and SGLang with Implications for Biomedical AI Agents and Bioinformatics Pipeline Developmen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13884)
*Jiada Li,Xuesong Ye,Olamide Olowoniyi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文对vLLM（18,290个PR）和SGLang（14,938个PR）两个高性能AI基础设施开源仓库进行了纵向分析，考察2023-2026年间七个工程指标的变化。研究发现两者开发速度大幅提升（PR吞吐量分别增长21倍和17.9倍），且增长主要来自人类开发者（机器人PR占比<0.2%）；评论密度增加4.2倍和3.8倍；作者数量和贡献者参与度稳步增长，而PR大小保持稳定。研究将开发分为四个时代，表明AI辅助开发与更高的吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与和更强的AI开发者协作信号相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程助手和自主智能体开发系统的快速普及与开源软件工程速度和结构的重要变化相吻合，但团队层面的经验性纵向证据仍然有限。该研究旨在弥补这一实证空白，通过系统性的纵向分析来量化AI辅助开发对开源团队工程指标的具体影响。

Method: 研究采用描述性纵向分析方法，计算了来自两个高速度AI基础设施仓库（vLLM和SGLang）所有合并PR的七个工程指标：PR吞吐量、周期时间、贡献者多样性、PR评论密度、合并率、新作者参与度和PR大小。该研究将开发过程划分为与AI辅助软件开发重大变化相一致的四个时代，并分别考察了人类和机器人生成的活动。

Result: 两个项目均显示出开发速度和AI开发者协作信号的显著增加。PR吞吐量在vLLM中增长21倍，在SGLang中增长17.9倍，但机器人PR仅占增长的不到0.2%，表明增长主要由人类驱动。最新时代中，vLLM和SGLang的中位周期时间分别为1.04天和0.62天，P90周期时间分别为16.8天和14.3天。每月唯一作者数量稳步增加，评论密度增加4.2倍（vLLM）和3.8倍（SGLang），机器人评论贡献了约15-20%的增长。PR大小在各时代间保持相对稳定。

Conclusion: AI辅助开发与高速度开源软件开发中的更高吞吐量、更广泛的贡献者参与以及增强的AI开发者协作信号相关联。研究提供了团队层面的经验证据，表明AI工具的采用正在重塑开源软件工程实践，同时人类开发者仍是推动这些变化的主导力量。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of AI coding assistants and autonomous agentic development systems has coincided with major changes in the pace and structure of open-source software engineering. Yet empirical longitudinal evidence of these changes at the team level remains limited. We present a descriptive longitudinal analysis of seven engineering metrics: pull request (PR) throughput, cycle time, contributor diversity, PR comment density, merge rate, new-author participation, and PR size. Metrics were computed from all merged PRs in two high-velocity AI infrastructure repositories, vLLM (February 2023-June 2026; 18,290 PRs) and SGLang (January 2024-June 2026; 14,938 PRs). We segment development into four eras aligned with major changes in AI-assisted software development and examine human- and bot-authored activities. Both projects show substantial increases in development velocity and AI-developer collaboration signals. PR throughput increased 21x in vLLM and 17.9x in SGLang, while bot-authored PRs accounted for less than 0.2% of this growth, indicating that the increase was overwhelmingly human-driven. In the latest era, median cycle time was 1.04 days for vLLM and 0.62 days for SGLang, while P90 cycle times reached 16.8 and 14.3 days, respectively. Monthly unique authors increased steadily in both projects, suggesting broader contributor participation. PR comment density increased 4.2x in vLLM and 3.8x in SGLang, with bot comments contributing an estimated 15-20% of the increase. In contrast, PR size remained relatively stable across eras. Overall, AI-assisted development is associated with higher throughput, broader contributor participation, and increased AI-developer collaboration signals in high-velocity open-source software development.

</details>


### [196] [Rethinking Automated Program Repair: The Impact of Bug Complexity, Fault Localization, and LLM Cost-efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14065)
*Junchi Liu,Ali Bigdeli,Roya Daneshi,Atu Ambala,Sudipto Ghosh,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对基于大语言模型(LLM)的自动程序修复(APR)技术进行了全面的实证分析，评估了两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector）配合三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）在不同bug复杂度和故障定位策略下的修复性能，并深入分析了推理设置与修复成本效益之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM-based APR研究主要关注整体修复效果，而忽略了bug复杂度、故障定位精度、推理设置和修复成本效益等关键因素对修复性能的影响，这些方面尚未得到充分探索。

Method: 采用多维度实证框架和统计分析，评估两种APR技术（ChatRepair和CodeCorrector）在三种LLM（DeepSeek、GPT、Llama）上的表现，考察不同bug复杂度水平和故障定位策略对修复效果的影响。

Result: 结构复杂的bug和不精确的故障定位会增加修复难度，但LLM-based APR仍能达到有竞争力的修复效果。不精确的故障定位会显著扩大APR技术之间的性能差距。更高成本的LLM和更强的推理设置并不总能带来更好的成本效益。GPT-5比DeepSeek-V4-pro和DeepSeek-V3.2分别多修复7个和39个复杂bug，但DeepSeek-V3.2的总修复成本效益最佳。

Conclusion: 超过50%的中等复杂度bug可以通过低成本的LLM-based APR技术修复。APR技术之间的修复效果差距随故障定位精度下降而增大，揭示了修复效果与计算成本之间存在非平凡的权衡关系。

Abstract: Background: Software bugs remain a critical challenge in development, necessitating effective Automated Program Repair (APR) techniques. While Large Language Model (LLM)-based APR systems have shown promise, prior studies primarily focus on overall repair effectiveness. The effects of bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and repair cost-effectiveness remain insufficiently explored.
  Aims: This study presents a comprehensive empirical analysis of LLM-based APR, focusing on how repair performance is shaped by bug complexity, fault localization, reasoning settings, and costs.
  Method: We evaluate two APR techniques (ChatRepair and CodeCorrector) using three LLMs (DeepSeek, GPT, and Llama), and examine their performance across diverse levels of bug complexity and localization strategies through a multi-dimensional empirical framework and statistical analysis.
  Results: Although structurally complex bugs and imprecise fault localization make repair more challenging, LLM-based APR techniques still achieve competitive repair effectiveness. Imprecise fault localization can substantially enlarge the performance gap between APR techniques. Furthermore, higher-cost LLMs and stronger reasoning settings do not consistently yield better cost-efficiency, revealing a nontrivial trade-off between repair effectiveness and computational cost.
  Conclusions: Over 50% of moderately complex bugs can be repaired by low-cost LLM-based APR techniques. The repair effectiveness gap between APR techniques becomes larger as fault localization becomes less precise. GPT-5 repairs 7 and 39 more complex bugs than DeepSeek-V4-pro and DeepSeek-V3.2, respectively; whereas the total repair cost of DeepSeek-V3.2 shows the best cost-efficiency performance.

</details>


### [197] [DepWareTrans: Dependency-Aware Incremental Repository Migration across Co-executable Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14128)
*Sivajeet Chand,Alexander Pretschner,Steve Haupt,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种依赖感知的增量迁移框架，将代码翻译单元从单个文件提升到依赖一致的批次，通过构建依赖图、分组互依赖文件并进行批量翻译与迭代验证，解决仓库级代码翻译中因跨文件依赖不一致导致的失败问题。在51K行工业系统及多种互操作语言对上实现100%编译与测试成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的代码翻译方法仅在文件级别操作，无法扩展至具有复杂跨文件依赖的代码库。在工业场景中（将STAR仓库从Java迁移至Kotlin），文件级方法产生碎片化结果且无法实现端到端正确性，核心原因是依赖不一致问题。

Method: 提出依赖感知的增量迁移框架：(1) 构建依赖图； (2) 将互依赖的文件分组为依赖一致的批次； (3) 对批次执行批量翻译； (4) 通过迭代的编译驱动和测试驱动验证进行校验与收敛。

Result: 在51K LOC工业系统（STAR）及多个可互操作语言对（Java-Kotlin、Java-Scala、C#-F#）上评估。文件级方法在STAR上仅达到38.16%编译成功率和9.39%测试成功率，而所提方法在所有评估设置下均达到100%编译和测试成功率，并在少量迭代内收敛。

Conclusion: 依赖感知的批量分组能够显著提升仓库级代码翻译的可扩展性与可靠性，且经验研究表明大部分错误源于无法通过迭代反馈单独解决的跨文件依赖问题。

Abstract: Repository-level code translation is critical for modernizing legacy systems, yet existing approaches based on large language models (LLMs) operate at the file level and fail to scale to codebases with complex inter-file dependencies. This limitation is evident in our industrial setting, where we aim to migrate a production repository (STAR) from Java to Kotlin, but file-level approaches produce fragmented results and fail to achieve end-to-end correctness. In this paper, we show that the primary cause of failure at the repository level is dependency inconsistency. Through an empirical study on open-source and industrial systems, we find that most errors arise from unresolved cross-file dependencies that cannot be effectively addressed by iterative feedback alone. We propose a dependency-aware incremental migration framework that elevates the unit of translation from individual files to dependency-consistent batches. Our approach constructs a dependency graph, groups interdependent files, and performs batched translation with iterative compile- and test-driven validation. We evaluate our method on a 51K line of code (LOC) industrial system and multiple repositories across interoperable language pairs (Java-Kotlin, Java-Scala, and C#-F#). On the STAR repository, file-level approaches achieve 38.16% compilation and 9.39% test success, whereas our approach achieves 100% compilation and test success across the evaluated settings, converging within a small number of iterations. These results show that dependency-aware batching improves scalability and reliability in repository-level code translation.

</details>


### [198] [LegacyWorld: Atomicity-Aware Evaluation of GUI Agents for Legacy Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14131)
*Thilo Reintjes,Sivajeet Chand,Derui Zhu,Sushant Kumar Pandey,Alexander Pretschner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一项针对legacy系统GUI工作流自动化框架（legacy-use）的预部署评估研究。研究提出用原子性（atomicity）作为评估标准——即代理运行要么完整正确完成工作流，要么失败且不留下持久副作用。作者构建了28个Windows GUI工作流的基准测试集，每个工作流包含初始状态、目标状态和特定验证器，并对比了专家手写提示与从屏幕录制中生成的提示，测试了六种托管计算机使用代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遗留企业系统难以现代化，关键工作流缺乏可编程接口，需要手动GUI交互。虽然多模态LLM代理可以自动化这些工作流，但成功的演示并不足够——失败的代理运行可能在业务或医疗记录中留下持久无效更改。因此需要评估代理运行的原子性，确保失败不会造成意外的持久副作用。

Method: 构建了一个由领域专家提供信息的28个Windows GUI工作流基准测试集，每个工作流指定初始状态、目标状态和任务特定验证器。对比两类提示：专家精心编写的提示与从专家黄金路径执行屏幕录制中自动生成的提示。在六种托管计算机使用代理上进行系统评估，关注有用完成、安全失败和非原子副作用三个操作维度。

Result: 六种托管计算机使用代理的评估结果表明，有用完成、安全失败和非原子副作用是三个截然不同的操作特征。工作流捕获、状态验证器和原子性感知的验收测试应成为AI遗留工作流自动化的首要需求。

Conclusion: 论文得出结论：对于AI驱动的遗留工作流自动化，应该将工作流捕获、状态验证器和原子性感知的验收测试作为一等公民需求。这一发现对工业生产环境中安全部署多模态LLM代理具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Legacy and legacy-like enterprise systems often remain difficult to modernize because critical workflows expose limited programmable interfaces and still require manual GUI interaction. This paper reports a pre-deployment evaluation study motivated by the development of legacy-use, an industry-oriented framework for automating such workflows with multimodal LLM agents. During framework development, domain experts helped identify stateful workflows where successful demos are not sufficient: a failed agent run may still leave persistent invalid changes in business or healthcare records. We therefore evaluate computer-use agents using atomicity: a run should either complete the intended workflow correctly or fail without unintended persistent side effects. We construct a domain-expert-informed benchmark of 28 Windows GUI workflows, each specified with an initial state, goal state, and task-specific validator. We compare expert-crafted prompts with prompts generated from screen recordings of expert golden-path executions. Across six hosted computer-use agents, our results show that useful completion, safe failure, and non-atomic side effects are distinct operational profiles. We conclude that workflow capture, state validators, and atomicity-aware acceptance tests should be first-class requirements for AI-based legacy workflow automation.

</details>


### [199] [Breaking Models to Test the Judge: A Mutation Testing Approach for Semantic Evaluators of Domain Class Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14315)
*Kevin Delcourt,Meriem Ben Chaaben,Abdelhamid Rouatbi,Luciano Marchezan,Houari Sahraoui*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变异测试（mutation testing）的方法，用于评估语义判断器（如基于LLM的judge）在领域类图与文本描述一致性判断任务中的表现。通过向PlantUML类图中注入受控语义缺陷（定义了11种变异算子），以候选判断器能否检测出缺陷作为评估标准，并与人工评估对比，结果在6种判断器配置下高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中的许多语义建模任务缺乏唯一的地面真值（ground truth），人工评判既昂贵又主观。需要一种可扩展的自动化替代方案来评估语义判断器的有效性。

Method: 1) 从PlantUML类图与文本系统描述对出发；2) 应用11种变异算子（如删除一个类）注入受控语义缺陷，生成错误变体；3) 候选判断器（不同LLM与提示词组合）被评估其检测注入缺陷的能力；4) 将自动化变异测试结果与人工评估的有效性进行对比。

Result: 在6种判断器配置（3种LLM × 2种提示词变体）下，自动化变异测试方法在识别性能更优配置方面与人工评估结果基本一致。

Conclusion: 变异测试可作为分析语义判断器的可扩展代理（scalable proxy），在缺乏人工标注时提供可靠的自动化评估途径。

Abstract: In software engineering, many semantic modeling tasks lack a unique ground truth, as human judgments are both costly and subjective. This paper explores mutation testing as a scalable alternative for evaluating semantic judges (e.g., LLM-based) of models. We propose a mutation testing approach in which controlled semantic defects are injected into domain class diagrams. Starting from pairs of PlantUML class diagrams and textual system descriptions, we apply mutation operators (e.g., removing a class) to generate faulty variants. A candidate judge is then evaluated based on its ability to detect the injected defects. We define 11 mutation operators for the task of comparing a domain class diagram against a textual description and evaluate the proposed approach against a conventional manual assessment of judgment validity. Across six judge configurations (three LLMs and two prompt variants), the automated mutation testing approach is largely consistent with the manual assessment in identifying the better-performing configurations. The results suggest that mutation testing may serve as a scalable proxy for analyzing semantic judges.

</details>


### [200] [ATLAS: Discovering Agent Strategies through LLM-Guided Abstraction and Automata Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14352)
*Ignacio D. Lopez-Miguel,Andreas Happe,Jürgen Cito,Ezio Bartocci,Bettina Könighofer,Martin Tappler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ATLAS方法，通过结合轨迹抽象与自动机学习，从LLM智能体的执行轨迹中恢复可解释的有限状态行为模型，以理解和分析智能体的行为策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在复杂任务中表现强大，但其行为难以理解、解释和分析。现有评估主要关注任务成功率和执行轨迹，对智能体所采用策略的洞察有限。

Method: ATLAS结合轨迹抽象（trace abstraction）与自动机学习（automata learning），从智能体与环境交互的轨迹中推断有限状态模型，捕捉行为策略，并支持符号模型转换及知识迁移。

Result: 在渗透测试智能体的轨迹上验证，ATLAS能暴露从原始轨迹难以识别的高层行为策略；实现从大型前沿模型到紧凑语言模型的符号模型知识迁移；在12台脆弱机器的案例研究中成功推导出简洁的行为解释。

Conclusion: ATLAS开辟了模型驱动工程的新机会，将智能体轨迹转化为显式行为模型，实现对不透明AI智能体的系统化理解与分析，支持可解释性、模型引导探索、审计等应用。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based agents are increasingly used for complex tasks such as software testing and cybersecurity assessment. While these agents demonstrate impressive capabilities, their behavior is difficult to understand, explain, and analyze. Existing evaluations focus mainly on task success and execution traces, offering limited insight into the strategies employed by the agent. We present ATLAS (Automata Learning for Agent Trajectory Analysis and Strategy Discovery), an approach for recovering interpretable behavioral models from agent trajectories. ATLAS combines trace abstraction with automata learning to infer finite-state models that capture observed agent-environment interaction strategies. These models provide human-interpretable insights and support automated analyses of recurring behaviors, decision points, successful task-completion paths, and failure loops. As a proof of concept, we apply ATLAS to trajectories generated by an LLM-based penetration-testing agent. The resulting models expose high-level behavioral strategies for exploiting vulnerable machines that are difficult to identify from raw execution traces alone. We discuss how learned behavioral models can support explainability, model-guided exploration, auditing, and analysis of agentic systems. We further demonstrate symbolic model-based knowledge transfer from powerful frontier models to compact language models. In addition, we show how model transformations can derive concise explanations of agent behavior in a penetration-testing case study comprising 12 vulnerable machines. ATLAS highlights a new opportunity for model-driven engineering: transforming agent trajectories into explicit behavioral models that enable systematic understanding and analysis of otherwise opaque AI agents.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [201] [Audience capture, selective exposure, affective assimilation or ideological sorting? Polarisation of climate politics under low media-party parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13864)
*Arttu Malkamäki,Antti Gronow*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析2015-2023年芬兰推特上不同意识形态阵营用户对新闻文章的发布和评论行为，检验了四种机制（受众捕获、选择性暴露、情感同化、意识形态排序），以解释在主流媒体不系统与党派立场对齐的体系中，气候政治极化如何发生。贝叶斯回归模型发现媒体选择分歧加剧（符合受众捕获）、选择性暴露部分支持、情感同化适度支持，而意识形态排序较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将气候政治极化归因于保守派与自由派媒体的不同立场，但这一解释仅适用于主流媒体与政党立场系统性对齐的多数决民主国家。作者旨在探究在主流媒体不系统对齐党派立场（如芬兰）的体系中，极化现象如何产生，从而填补理论空白。

Method: 分析芬兰推特（2015-2023）上不同意识形态阵营用户的新闻发布与评论行为，构建贝叶斯回归模型检验四种极化机制：受众捕获（媒体适应差异化受众）、选择性暴露（用户发布意识形态一致的文章）、情感同化（同一内容以意识形态一致的效价传播）、意识形态排序（跨议题的身份驱动一致行为）。

Result: 发现媒体选择分歧随时间加剧，保守倾向用户更常发布小报内容（支持受众捕获）；部分支持选择性暴露（同一媒体内文章发布日益分化）；情感效价分歧仅轻微增加（情感同化适度支持）；特定议题社区与阵营一致性较弱，但随时间趋于融合。

Conclusion: 研究精炼了多数决民主之外的媒体效应理论，为理解当前气候政治僵局提供了新视角，表明即使媒体生态不同，极化仍可通过受众与媒体间的反馈循环等机制发展。

Abstract: Studies often attribute the polarisation of climate politics in majoritarian democracies to differing treatment of climate change by conservative and liberal media. How then does such polarisation occur in a system, where mainstream media do not systematically align with party positions? Focusing on how users in different ideological blocs posted and commented on news articles on Finnish Twitter between 2015 and 2023, we address four mechanisms: audience capture, where outlets adapt to increasingly differentiated audiences, reinforcing their divergence through feedback between audience engagement and editorial incentives; selective exposure, where users post ideologically congruent articles from the same outlets; affective assimilation, where users communicate identical content with ideology-consistent valence; and ideological sorting, where identity-driven alignment structures news-posting behaviour across multiple issues. Bayesian regression models indicate increasing divergence in outlet choice, with conservative-leaning users posting more tabloid content, in line with audience capture, and partial support for selective exposure, as within-outlet article posting becomes increasingly differentiated. Valence divergence increases only slightly over time, providing moderate support for affective assimilation, while issue-specific communities show weak alignment with blocs despite convergence over time. Our work refines theories of media effects beyond majoritarian democracies and contributes to understanding the current impasse in climate politics.

</details>


### [202] [Whose Posts Get Ranked: Identical-Text Exposure Gaps in Bluesky Custom Feeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13879)
*Yipeng Wang,Mohit Singhal*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究Bluesky平台上的自定义信息流（feeds）是否对相同文本的内容给予同等曝光。通过对1,366个公共feed的Top-50列表进行重复快照，并将相同文本、不同作者、发布时间接近的帖子分组比较，发现33%的匹配集中出现一篇帖子在列表上而另一篇不在的情况。固定效应回归表明，这种曝光差异与作者在该feed上的历史记录相关：新作者的曝光更少，而曾在该feed上被返回过的作者曝光更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bluesky允许用户部署自定义feed（独立运营的推荐算法），平台同时提供数千个这样的feed。一个关键的开放问题是：这些独立feed是否能让相同文本获得平等曝光？研究者希望检验"feed多样性是否能保证内容公平"这一假设。

Method: 对1,366个公共feed的Top-50列表进行重复快照采样；将相同文本、不同作者、在同一列表响应前创建且年龄相近的帖子分组为匹配集；使用固定效应回归分析曝光差异与作者历史行为的关系。

Result: 匹配集覆盖250个feed，其中33%的集合中一篇帖子出现在列表上而另一篇没有。新作者在相同文本下获得的曝光更少（倒数排名权重-0.061），而有过被返回历史的作者获得更多曝光。新作者即使粉丝更多，仍在74%的头对头比较中落败。媒体和帖子类型特征在校正多重比较后未发现显著关联。

Conclusion: 这些结果提供早期证据表明，即使访问众多独立feed，也不足以让相同文本获得平等曝光。feed的推荐行为可能存在对作者的"冷启动"偏差或历史偏好效应。

Abstract: Bluesky lets users deploy custom feeds, independently operated recommendation algorithms that the platform serves alongside thousands of others. This paper investigates how evenly these feeds treat posts with the same text. To measure this, we take repeated snapshots of the Top-50 lists that 1,366 public feeds return, and we group posts with identical text, from different authors, that were created before the same list response and closely matched in age. Exposure diverges widely inside these matched sets, which span 250 feeds: in 33% of sets, one copy appears on the list while another does not. Fixed-effects regressions show that this divergence is associated with the author's history on the specific feed. Authors new to a feed receive less exposure for the same text (-0.061 in reciprocal-rank weight), while authors whose posts the feed has returned before receive more. A new author with more followers than the competing author still loses 74\% of head-to-head comparisons. Media and post-type features show no detectable association after multiple-comparison correction. These results are early evidence that access to many independent feeds is not enough to give identical texts equal exposure.

</details>


### [203] [Spectral Efficiency Centrality: An Efficient Spectral Approach for Influential Node Identification in Temporal Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13960)
*Aksa Urooj,Iqra Altaf Gillani*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时间谱中心性框架——谱效率中心性（SEC），通过评估节点移除对时间快照中谱半径的改变来衡量节点重要性，并基于Perron-Frobenius理论和一阶特征值扰动开发了高效近似算法ASEC，在SI、SIS和IC扩散模型下优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间中心性度量主要依赖局部结构属性或时间路径，而谱节点移除方法仅限于静态网络，缺乏能够在时间演化网络中捕捉全局结构影响的谱中心性框架。

Method: 提出SEC框架，通过计算节点移除后时间快照中谱半径的变化来量化节点重要性；进一步基于Perron-Frobenius理论和一阶特征值扰动开发ASEC近似算法，仅需前导特征对，避免重复特征分解，提高计算可扩展性。

Result: 在多个真实时间网络数据集上的广泛实验表明，SEC和ASEC在SI、SIS和IC扩散模型下识别有影响力节点方面优于现有基线中心性度量，统计显著性和鲁棒性分析进一步验证了其有效性，ASEC在大规模时间网络上具有计算高效性。

Conclusion: SEC和ASEC为时间网络中识别有影响力节点提供了一种全局谱视角的新框架，兼顾了识别精度与计算效率，适用于大规模时间演化网络。

Abstract: Centrality measures play a vital role in identifying influential nodes in evolving networks. While existing temporal centrality measures primarily rely on local structural properties or temporal paths, spectral node-removal approaches have been largely limited to static networks. To bridge this gap, we propose Spectral Efficiency Centrality (SEC), a temporal spectral centrality framework that quantifies node importance by evaluating the change in spectral radius caused by node removal across temporal snapshots. By capturing the global structural influence of nodes throughout network evolution, SEC identifies nodes that are critical for preserving the structural connectivity and efficiency of temporal networks. To improve computational scalability, we further develop an efficient approximation, ASEC, based on Perron-Frobenius theory and first-order eigenvalue perturbation. ASEC requires only the leading eigenpair and avoids repeated eigendecomposition, making it suitable for large temporal networks. Extensive experiments on multiple real-world temporal datasets demonstrate that SEC and ASEC outperform existing baseline centrality measures in identifying influential nodes under SI, SIS, and IC diffusion models. Statistical significance and robustness analyses further confirm their effectiveness, while ASEC offers a computationally efficient solution for large-scale temporal networks.

</details>


### [204] [RegRole: Regularized Role Detection and Prediction in Temporal Dynamic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14504)
*Emily J Evans,Weihong Guo,Carlotta Domenicon*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间正则化非负矩阵分解(NMF)的动态角色发现技术，能够在时间动态网络中创建跨所有时间段一致的角色集合及通用转移矩阵，并对角色成员变化施加正则化惩罚以增强鲁棒性。在五个真实数据集和一个合成数据集上的实验表明，该方法能降低预测误差，产生更稳定的系统（个体更倾向于留在原角色），且能高效扩展到大规模稀疏图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动态角色分析技术缺乏跨时间段的一致性处理，容易受到真实世界噪声的影响，角色成员在时间期间可能发生剧烈变化，需要一种更稳健且统一的动态角色发现方法。

Method: 利用时间正则化的NMF方法，创建跨所有时间段一致的角色集合以及一个通用的角色间转移概率矩阵；同时施加正则化惩罚项，约束角色成员关系在不同时间段之间不会发生剧烈变化。

Result: 在五个真实数据集和一个合成数据集上，使用工程化特征和自动生成特征进行测试，发现选择合适的正则化权重参数可降低预测误差；转移矩阵的迹分析表明该方法产生更稳定的系统，个体更倾向于停留在原角色中，任意转移更少。

Conclusion: 该模型能学习时间对齐的角色，捕捉行为随时间的转移，同时高效扩展到大规模稀疏图，为动态网络中的角色分析提供了更稳健和一致的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper introduces a dynamic role discovery technique in temporal dynamic networks, utilizing temporally regularized Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF). Our technique differs from existing dynamic role analysis techniques by creating a consistent set of roles across all time periods, as well as a universal transition matrix that describes the probability of transitioning between roles. We also apply a regularization penalty to ensure that role membership does not change dramatically between time periods making our model more robust against real-world noise. We test our data on five real-world and one synthetically simulated dataset using both engineered and automatically generated features. We demonstrate that the proposed regularized role detection method, for appropriate regularization weight parameter reduces prediction errors compared to other techniques. Furthermore, trace analysis of the transition matrices indicates that our method yields a more stable system, that is, individuals are more likely to stay in their roles with fewer arbitrary transitions. Our model learns time-aligned roles, captures behavioral transitions over time, and scales efficiently to large and sparse graphs.

</details>
